\hypertarget{introduction}{%
\section{Introduction}\label{introduction}}

👉 Start up the \textbf{Vibe} and get confident with this User Guide and with Compulite Support.

This chapter discusses getting started with Compulite Vibe.

\textbf{Chapter index:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \protect\hyperlink{Start-Up-1.1}{1.1 Start Up}
\item
  \protect\hyperlink{Guide-Conventions-1.2}{1.2 Guide Conventions}

  \protect\hyperlink{Important-Terms-1.3}{1.3 Important Terms}

\hypertarget{Start-Up-1.1}{%
\subsection{Start Up}\label{Start-Up-1.1}}

\textbf{Power requirements:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Vibe uses a power supply that supports 90-264V at 47-63 Hz.
\item
  Approximately power consumption is 3.2A at 230V.
\item
  Input uses a Neutrik powerCON connector.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Desk Light:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Insert the goose neck desk light into the 3 pin XLR on the rear of the console.
\item
  Desk light intensity is controlled in the \{System Settings\} \{Hardware\} tab.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Monitor intensity:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Monitor intensity is controlled in the \{System Settings\} \{Hardware\} tab.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Power up the console:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Insert the supplied powerCON male connector into the female connector located on the rear of the console. Align the guide slots and turn clockwise untill the connector locks.
\item
  Turn on the power supply switch above the powerCON connector.
\item
  Press the Power button located at the upper corner of the monitor panel.
\item
  The operating system will boot and the Vibe console software will automatically launch.
\end{itemize}

A loading popup with a countdown asks if the user wants to open a New Show or the Current Show.
If no selection is made the Current Show is loaded.

\begin{quote}
After opening the Vibe software for the first time, the Tutorial popup is shown. This option can be disabled through the ``Don't Show Again'' key.
\end{quote}

\textbf{Power down the console:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Tap the \{Vibe\} menu key beside the command line on the main display - The Vibe Menu will open.
\item
  Tap \{Exit\} - The \{Exit Vibe\} popup will appear.
\item
  Tap \{Yes\} or \{No\} to save or not the current show file.
\item
  The Vibe application will shutdown.
\item
  Press the Power button - The console will safely power off.
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{Guide-Conventions-1.2}{%
\subsection{Guide Conventions}\label{Guide-Conventions-1.2}}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{{[}XXXX{]}} - Physical key
\item
  \textbf{\{XXXX\}} - Soft key
\item
  \textbf{{[} {]}} \textbf{\{ \}} - Example
\item
  \textbf{Press} - Action applied to a physical key
\item
  \textbf{Tap} - Action applied to a softkey or a virtual key
\item
  \textbf{Toggle} - Action that actives and deactivates with alternating taps or presses
\item
  \textbf{Key} - Any physical or virtual button
\item
  \textbf{Keypad} - Embedded numeric keypad
\item
  \textbf{Keyboard} - Console's pull-out ASCII keyboard or any external keyboard
\item
  \textbf{SK -} Object softkeys (Libraries, Groups, Snaps, Macros, etc.)
\item
  👉 \textbf{-} Hint or suggestion
\item
  {\textbf{RED}} \textbf{-} Important information or Caution
\item
  \textbf{{[}Here{]}} - Short form for Press an appropriate destination key or button
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{Important-Terms-1.3}{%
\subsection{Important Terms}\label{Important-Terms-1.3}}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{DMX 512 Output/Input Port} - One of the 8 physical 5 pin XLR connectors on the back of the console. Although the gender is female, any connector may be configured as an input but must use a turnaround adapter.
\item
  \textbf{DMX 512 Universe} - Virtual DMX outputs 0 ⟶ 256 that may be routed to the 8 physical outputs and/or any of the three supported DMX over Ethernet protocols.

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Compulite VC
  \item
    ArtNet
  \item
    sACN
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{HTP (Highest-Takes-Precedence)} - When the same parameters are on two or more controllers, the highest value will output to the stage. Mainly used for dimmer values.
\item
  \textbf{LTP (Latest-Takes-Precedence)} - When the same parameters are on two or more controllers, the last asserted parameter will output it's values to the stage, no matter what the value is.
\item
  \textbf{Show} - The file saved by the software containing all the programmed data.
\item
  \textbf{Layout} - The file saved by the software containing all the visualization data.
\item
  \textbf{Window} - An element of the layout that contains up to 12 Pages. When using multiple screens, each one can have its own window.
\item
  \textbf{Page} - An element of the window that contains the Views. Vibe has three page types called \textbf{Patch}, \textbf{Prog}, \textbf{Play}.
\item
  \textbf{View} - A single element that can be added to a Page, such as universe view, live view, timeline view, topo view, qlist tracksheet, and many others.
\item
  \textbf{HardKey} - Any physical button on the console or its virtual equivalent on the screen.
\item
  \textbf{SoftKey} - Any virtual button on the screen that corresponds to a stored element such as libraries, groups, qlists, scenes, macros, and snaps.
\item
  \textbf{Set} = Fixture numbering system

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    0 = Fixtures, (Default, includes all sets)
  \item
    1 = Channels (Conventional fixtures)
  \item
    2 = Spots (Automated heads and mirrors)
  \item
    3 = Matrix (LED pixel-based fixtures)
  \item
    4 = Servers (Video servers)
  \item
    5 ⟶ 1000 = User defined sets
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Fixture} - An independent object controlled by the console thorough DMX or network protocol, such as a conventional or LED luminaire, a moving head, or a media server.
\item
  \textbf{Parameter} - An individual feature of a fixture such as dimmer, pan, tilt, color wheel, gobo wheel.
\item
  \textbf{Bank} - A group of features of a fixture such as intensity, position, color, beam, image and shape.
\item
  \textbf{Encoder} - A physical or virtual wheel used to control the values of the fixtures' parameters.
\item
  \textbf{Editor} - The part of the system that is used to program the fixtures and store the objects.
\item
  \textbf{Playback} - The part of the system that is used to play the fixtures by triggering the stored objects.
\item
  \textbf{Smart Screen} - A view on the Vibe software that controls the Editor and every parameter of the selected fixtures. In 2-screen consoles the Smart Screen is always shown on the small screen on the right side.
\item
  \textbf{Controller} - Any physical or virtual control element that executes or plays back system objects such as cues, scenes, macros, and snapshots.
\item
  \textbf{Black Out} - A physical or virtual button that inhibits the dimmer values of every fixture bringing them at 0\%, often referred to as a \textbf{B.O.}.
\item
  \textbf{Grand Master} - A physical or virtual control element that inhibits the dimmer values of every fixture, proportionate to their assigned value.
\item
  \textbf{Submaster} - A special type of Scene where stored fixtures inhibit their dimmer values, proportionate to their assigned value.
\item
  \textbf{Master Controller} - Main playback section of the console with large {[}GO{]}, {[}BACK{]}, {[}HOLD{]} keys. There can be only one Master Controller, but it can be assigned to any controller.
\item
  \textbf{Playback Master} - A controller that is mastering other controllers.
\item
  \textbf{Slider} - A slider potentiometer Controller, often referred to as a \textbf{Fader}.
\item
  \textbf{Qkey} - A single button or a knob potentiometer Controller.
\item
  \textbf{Auxkey} - A single button potentiometer Controller.
\item
  \textbf{Global} - A slider potentiometer Controller.
\item
  \textbf{CrossFader} - A pair of slider potentiometer Controllers used to manually control the fade in and fade out of consecutive Cues.
\item
  \textbf{TrackBall} - Ay physical control element with a spheric shape used to focus moving fixtures through the Position parameters of Pan and Tilt.
\item
  \textbf{Qlist} - A Folder containing cues. Each Qlist has its own unique numbering system from 0.001 to 999.999.
\item
  \textbf{Chaser} - A Qlist that is set to run automatically into a loop.
\item
  \textbf{Cue} - A record contained within a Qlist. Cues contain stored parameter values and properties such as text label and time values.
\item
  \textbf{Cue Zero} - The starting point at the top of a Qlist. Release may or may not return to the top of a Qlist depending on Qlist Properties. Cue Zero will always return to the top of the Qlist. The command Cue 0 GOTO is valid.
\item
  \textbf{Scene} - A record that has the same properties of a Cue but exists independently from a Qlist.
\item
  \textbf{Library} - Container of frequently used fixture parameter values (Bank Library) or system objects (Object Library) . Bank Libraries may be created as referenced or unreferenced. The default for most libraries is referenced.
\item
  \textbf{Options} - Used to modify object properties ``on the fly''.
\item
  \textbf{Settings} - Used to modify object properties and behaviors after an object has been created.
\end{itemize}

\begin{quote}
👉 For the sake of this guide, \textbf{Channel, Spot, Matrix, and Server Sets} will be referred to as \textbf{Fixture} because the Fixture Set can reference all the other sets.
\end{quote}

\begin{quote}
👉 Some keys have two functions when used with the {[}VIBE{]} key (shift key). In this case, the key label on the bottom is the default, and the top label is the shifted function.
\end{quote}

\hypertarget{Software-Capacity-1.4}{%
\subsection{Software Capacity}\label{Software-Capacity-1.4}}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  256 DMX universes.
\item
  131072 DMX addresses.
\item
  1 physical/virtual CrossFader controller, with 2 non-motorized faders and 2 buttons.
\item
  5 physical/virtual Global Slider controllers with non-motorized slider and 1 button.
\item
  100 physical/virtual Slider Set controllers, with motorized fader and 3 buttons.
\item
  100 physical/virtual Qkey controllers with knob or single button.
\item
  100 physical/virtual AuxKey controllers with single button.
\item
  30 individual pages for Slider Sets, Qkeys, and AuxKeys.
\item
  9000+ total physical/virtual controllers.
\item
  1000 Qlists with virtually unlimited cues.
\item
  1000 Scenes.
\item
  1000 Fixture Groups.
\item
  1000 Libraries per each bank type.
\item
  1000 Effects.
\item
  1000 Snaps.
\item
  1000 Macros.
\item
  1000 Timelines.
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{Releases-1.6}{%
\subsection{Releases}\label{Releases-1.6}}

\hypertarget{r_7.0}{%
\subsubsection{R\_7.0}\label{r_7.0}}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Release Date: \textbf{07/07/2025}
\item
  DB version: \textbf{R\_7.0}
\item
  Desktop version: \textbf{R\_7.0}
\item
  LPU version: \textbf{R\_7.0}
\item
  HAL version: \textbf{R\_7.0}
\item
  Device Pack version: \textbf{DP\_7.0}
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{version-highlights}{%
\paragraph{Version Highlights}\label{version-highlights}}

Version R\_7.0 brings new and updated features as listed below:

\textbf{General}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Performance improvement} This software release features important background performance developments that improve the software elaboration speed.
\item
  \textbf{Startup popup} The startup process and the loading popup have been changed.
\item
  \textbf{Default layouts changed} The default layouts have been updated to include the latest views.
\item
  \textbf{Vibe Manual embedded} The Vibe Manual is now embedded in the Vibe Software.
\item
  \textbf{Drag views between pages} Views can be moved between different pages.
\item
  \textbf{Quick layout actions} The long click gesture can be used on toolbar and empty spaces for quick layout actions.
\item
  \textbf{Shift + Vibe Pages} Vibe Page buttons with Shift will sync the paging between all the Vibe screens.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Patch}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Auto Generate Shape Libraries} Shape Libraries can be automatically created with the Auto Generate feature.
\item
  \textbf{Calibration Data import} Calibration data can be imported from csv files.
\item
  \textbf{MVR import} Devices, Fixtures, Patch and Calibration data can be imported from MVR files.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Program}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Quick Actions on Wheel Encoders and Banks} The long click gesture on wheel encoders and extra wheels opens a quick actions box.
\item
  \textbf{Position params Lock} Position Lock functions allow to lock a movement direction.
\item
  \textbf{Copy / Paste params to Groups} Groups can be selected to Copy/Paste param values to other Groups.
\item
  \textbf{Copy / Paste Banks of params} Banks of params can be selected to Copy/Paste param values to Fixtures.
\item
  \textbf{Source PB on the Smart Screen} Smart Screen's Format options allow to show the Source Playback of the values.
\item
  \textbf{Copy \& Load Fixture from Cue} Selected Fixture values can be loaded or copied directly from target cues.
\item
  \textbf{Live View modes} Live View has 4 modes that can be switched from the Vibe menu or from the view settings.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Playback}

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Qlist Tracksheet menu redesign} Qlist Tracksheet view's menu has a more efficient way of filtering the information.
\item
  \textbf{Auxkey page up/down keys} Auxkey pages can be switched from the Smart Screen with the virtual up/down keys.
\item
  \textbf{GOTO \# GO} GOTO \# GO is now also a valid command that act like GOTO \# ENTER.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Settings}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Assign IP to General Networks} General Network IP addresses can be configured from the Network Settings popup.
\end{itemize}

-\textbf{Unicast on sACN} sACN protocol can be transmitted in Unicast.

\begin{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Local VC with 64 universes} Local VC in DMX settings popup allows configuring up to 64 universes.
\item
  \textbf{Vibe Wings memory settings} Vibe wings (including Vector compatible wings) remember the last configured settings.
\item
  \textbf{Vibe PC Wing functions disable} Vibe PC Wing functions can be disabled to inhibit the use of part of the panel.
\item
  \textbf{View Key screen color coding} The View Page key changes color according to the active screen.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Fixture Builder}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Reduced device DB size} The Device Database size has been reduced.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Offline Tool}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Opening check if Vibe is running} If Offline Tool is launched from the system a popup advice asks to close the Vibe first.
\end{itemize}

\hypertarget{bug-fixes}{%
\paragraph{Bug Fixes}\label{bug-fixes}}

\textbf{Vibe Crash Fixes}
- HAL could crash if a panel is connecting more than once
- Crash when having more than 1 small screen and changing to FX screen
- Crash when removing DMX Output view and then changing dmx settings
- LPU could get stuck if going back twice to empty (empty cue) in VColor values
- Crash when using LIBRARY 1 \#
- Qlist Tracksheet stuck/crash/performance
- Exam could crash when pressing on tabs
- Crash when trying to create shutter-beam-blade command while device has only 1 parameter
- Potential crashes of LPU during undo and other actions
- Crash when selecting fixtures on an old layout that had a Vibe image that does not exists anymore
- Crash with move controller syntax when combining numeric and controller selection
- Grab, Controller, Fixture Selection, Enter can crash the system
- Potential HAL crash when there is more than 1 console on the network
- Switching from selection to spinning while throwing wheel can cause a crash
- Switching modes fast from selection to spinning can cause the wheel to remain instead of the intended fader, and if moved it will crash

\textbf{Vibe Bug Fixes}
Performance
- Reduced memory usage
- General system speed
- Startup process
- Switching Topos performance
- Shift + Reset performance
- Grids loading (Live, Tracksheet etc.)
- Deleting many fixtures
- Improvement in Live view
- Improvement in Parameters view
- Renumber performance
- Fixture Rename performance
- Fixture Repatch performance
- Add/Remove devices/fixtures performance
- Fixture related operations performance
- Device Replacement performance
Layout
- Default layout should close windows if they were not in the default layout
- Vibe windows are not always located correct if there are external screens
- Live view anchor does not work in headers per device
- Some popups did not open correctly if they were first opened from an external screen.
Timeline
- Timeline Zoom and Scroll performance improvement
- Timeline View events are doubled when duplicating a timeline
Effects
- Library effects could make strange spread with the size
- Small Screen effect wheels were not always synced
- Big FX Screen wheels were not always synced
- Some Effect Wheels does not accept Hardkeys negative values
- Effect with grouping stores wrong selection index to lib
- Drag \& drop library in library effect popup was not working
Playback
- Values jump when cue time in/out and follow are cut with lookahead
- Qlist with only FX are not loaded
- Force Black on 1st Cue corrupts fade
- Wrong Wait time could cause chasers to get stuck
- Submaster with non-dimmer fixtures did not select the fixtures
- Fixed Prevent from paging issues with all controller types
- If a parameter had value lower than 0 (FX or Fan) and in the next cue it had cell time with hard value then the fade was wrong
- Cue number floating point inaccuracies
- Release Controller didn't work if it was prevented from paging and on a different page than the original
- PB Masters did not control any other controller than Sliders
- When a PB is going out to release (by release or back) with more than 1 cue in the ``stack'' then values can jump
- Snap load could load wrong state of Scenes
Blind
- Selection was not kept if changing between editor and blind
- Blind did not show the values if set from pickers
- Live view in blind mode did not show background colors on cells in edit
- Grab active parameters on Blind does not work
Time
- System time did not work correct with virtual parameters
- Cues with time/delay in/out in Cut and that also have follow time less than look ahead time could make jumps in playbacks
- Time values on views were not always rounded
Editing
- Parameter manipulation after RESET can cause some parameters to be stuck if working in non-grouped parameter mode
- Control Commands do not work when default slider is Rate
- Topo View selection skips second
- Topo Matrix Frame Selection - selecting same fixture many times
- Library Edit+Delete fix cues
- Move cues between qlists could miss deleting of src cues
- Apply library with layer selection did not work
- Apply Prebuild Effect with layer selection did not work
- Grab with layer selection did not work
- Beam picker showed only Normal mode and not strobe/custom
- Custom Library Update loses SK settings
- Auto generate added information to the library cells that could cause errors when updating
- Double click on empty topo space doesn't work good with group selection
- Update library issues when adding new fixtures
- Switching modes fast from selection to spinning can cause the spinning GUI to have buttons but no fader
- Release fixtures from library that isn't fixture specific was not working
- Next/Prev with a different set than Fixture was not working
- Cue Settings popup sometimes opens on default
- Live intensity icon can show full when dimmer value is close to 0
- Paste Cue from delta did not work
- Bug fixes in store/update/delete of records (cue, library, scene)
- Cue Only cue did not output the tracked cells
- Cue Only cue type did not act like Cue Only
- Changing cue type could cause wrong behavior
- Context View did not refresh when fixture rename
- Context View did not refresh when set change
- Scene Settings Popup can show wrong default name in the textbox when selecting a scene
- Device Replace / Clone - could have errors if there is a device with more than 1 dimmer
- Device Replace / Clone - could have errors if there is a device with more than 1 shutter
- Parameters that were active with FX from look ahead were not selected correctly in editor
- Release values from cues can fail if the same cue was selected more than once in the selection
- Release values from cues does not take .\# cues if they are in the range
Remotes
- Wing with an offset of sliders that was reconnected did not refresh its sliders position
- Connecting a wing with non-motorized sliders makes a jump in sliders on connection
Gestures
- Scroll gesture when fingers are too close can cause many button presses
- Scroll gesture fast on the side can cause many button presses
- Made the square notification of touches on consoles disappear
- Grid mouse scroll scrollbar doesn't disappear
- Fixture Data view drag selection was not working right
Park
- It was not possible to park virtual parameters correctly
- Parking physical parameters did not avoid using virtual and changing the values
Vector convert
- Could fail if access driver was not installed on the pc
- Redesigned convert popup and added more stages to select and configure object numbers
- Added an option to generate the conversion report as a sheet
- Effects with cycle time 0 did not work as expected
- Playbacks got ``no snap override'' option when they shouldn't have
- Similar effects with different sizes could be mistakenly combined
- Cells with effect from reference library did not get effect
- Tracked cells with effect did not work correctly
- Object numbers above 999 were not accessible on Vibe
- Auto match did only match for the first time
- Error value if resolution of parameter changed
- AutoBuild could make errors
- Topo + Matrix fix when multiple matrix-topo exist

\textbf{Fixture Builder Bug Fixes}
- Devices stored from FixtureBuilder will have broken Intensity visualization in Live View and Topo View
- Update bladeCommands param bank param properties
- BladeCmd - Create Other Blades can be created with bad data
- Gobo icons in LiveVIew, Topo, SmartScreen can have texture break when returning from FixtureBuilder
- Gobo/color icons in LiveVIew, Topo, SmartScreen can be not updated if gobo/color was changed in FixtureBuilder
- WheelCommands delete-clone may affect wrong cmd

\textbf{Offline Tool Bug Fixes}
- Software patch could not run more than 1 sql script if registry was not set right
- SW Update did not work correctly
